===== M0001 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2417-2426
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2417 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2418 |     int len = s.length();
2419 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2420 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2421 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2422 |         return false;
2423 |       }
2424 |     }
2425 |     return true;
2426 |   }

/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.
   *
   * @param operator the operator's token value to convert
   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator
   */
Method code:
2142 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
2143 |     switch (operator) {
2144 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
2145 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
2146 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
2147 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";
2148 |       case Token.BITAND: return "&";
2149 |       case Token.SHEQ: return "===";
2150 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
2151 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
2152 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
2153 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
2154 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";
2155 |       case Token.IN: return "in";
2156 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
2157 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
2158 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
2159 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
2160 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
2161 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
2162 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";
2163 |       case Token.DIV: return "/";
2164 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
2165 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
2166 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
2167 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
2168 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
2169 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
2170 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";
2171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR: return "|=";
2172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR: return "^=";
2173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";
2174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH: return "<<=";
2175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH: return ">>=";
2176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH: return ">>>=";
2177 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD: return "+=";
2178 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB: return "-=";
2179 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL: return "*=";
2180 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV: return "/=";
2181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD: return "%=";
2182 |       case Token.VOID: return "void";
2183 |       case Token.TYPEOF: return "typeof";
2184 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF: return "instanceof";
2185 |       default: return null;
2186 |     }
2187 |   }

241 |   public List<DiGraphNode<N, E>> getDirectedSuccNodes(
242 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode) {
243 |     if (dNode == null) {
244 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(dNode + " is null");

154 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
155 |     switch (n.getType()) {

176 |         return;
177 |

179 |       case Token.COMMA:

198 |       case Token.SUB:
199 |       case Token.ADD:
200 |       case Token.MUL:
201 |       case Token.MOD:
202 |       case Token.DIV:
203 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
204 |         return;
205 | 
206 |       // Assignments
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:

215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:

217 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:

219 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
220 |         return;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.HOOK:
223 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
224 |         return;
225 | 
226 |       // Node types that require special handling
227 |       case Token.STRING:
228 |         validateString(n);
229 |         return;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.NUMBER:
232 |         validateNumber(n);
233 |         return;
234 |

236 |         validateName(n);
237 |         return;
238 |

240 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
241 |         return;
242 | 
243 |       case Token.GETPROP:
244 |         validateGetProp(n);
245 |         return;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
248 |         validateArrayLit(n);
249 |         return;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         validateObjectLit(n);
253 |         return;

256 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
257 |         return;
258 |

260 |         validateCall(n);
261 |         return;
262 |

266 |

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 158-174
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
158 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
159 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
160 | 
161 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
162 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
163 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
164 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
165 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
166 |       }
167 | 
168 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
169 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
170 |       if (first.isName() &&
171 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
172 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
173 |       }
174 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.mayThrowException(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 910-931
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
910 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
911 |     switch (n.getType()) {
912 |       case Token.CALL:
913 |       case Token.GETPROP:
914 |       case Token.GETELEM:
915 |       case Token.THROW:
916 |       case Token.NEW:
917 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
918 |       case Token.INC:
919 |       case Token.DEC:
920 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
921 |         return true;
922 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
923 |         return false;
924 |     }
925 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
926 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
927 |         return true;
928 |       }
929 |     }
930 |     return false;
931 |   }

===== M0029 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,CompilerOptions,SourceMap,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 646-682
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to JS code
   */
Method code:
646 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
647 |       CompilerOptions options, SourceMap sourceMap,  boolean tagAsStrict) {
648 |     Preconditions.checkState(options.sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
649 | 
650 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
651 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
652 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
653 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
654 |             options.lineBreak,
655 |             options.preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile,
656 |             options.lineLengthThreshold,
657 |             createSourceMap,
658 |             options.sourceMapDetailLevel)
659 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
660 |             options.lineLengthThreshold,
661 |             createSourceMap,
662 |             options.sourceMapDetailLevel);
663 |     CodeGenerator cg =
664 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
665 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, options)
666 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, options);
667 | 
668 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
669 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
670 |     }
671 | 
672 |     cg.add(root);
673 |     mcp.endFile();
674 | 
675 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
676 | 
677 |     if (createSourceMap) {
678 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
679 |     }
680 | 
681 |     return code;
682 |   }

===== M0044 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 644-676
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
644 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
645 |       Preconditions.checkState(
646 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
647 |       Node previous = null;
648 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
649 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
650 |       // are already in the right place.
651 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
652 |         previous = current;
653 |         current = current.getNext();
654 |       }
655 | 
656 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
657 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
658 |       while (current != null) {
659 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
660 |         Node next = current.getNext();
661 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
662 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
663 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
664 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
665 | 
666 |           // Read the function at the top of the function body (after any
667 |           // previous declarations).
668 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
669 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
670 |         } else {
671 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
672 |           previous = current;
673 |         }
674 |         current = next;
675 |       }
676 |     }

===== M0050 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0060 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 274-337
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
274 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
275 |     this.options = options;
276 |     if (errorManager == null) {
277 |       if (outStream == null) {
278 |         setErrorManager(
279 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
280 |       } else {
281 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
282 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
283 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
284 |         setErrorManager(printer);
285 |       }
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
289 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
290 |       options.checkTypes = true;
291 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
292 |       options.checkTypes = false;
293 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
294 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
295 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
296 |       // parser type warnings.
297 |       options.setWarningLevel(
298 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
299 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
300 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
304 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
305 |       options.setWarningLevel(
306 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
307 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
308 |     }
309 | 
310 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
311 |       options.setWarningLevel(
312 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
313 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
314 |     }
315 | 
316 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
317 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
318 |     guards.add(
319 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
320 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
321 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
322 | 
323 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
324 | 
325 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
326 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
327 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
328 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
329 |     // error off.
330 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
331 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
332 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
333 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
337 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.

Method code:
111 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
112 |                                   String sourceString,
113 |                                   Config config,
114 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
115 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
116 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
117 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
118 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
119 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
120 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
121 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
122 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);

130 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;

132 |

135 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
136 |

138 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
139 |     try {
140 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
141 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
142 |       logger.info(
143 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
144 |     } finally {
145 |       Context.exit();

147 |     Node root = null;
148 |     if (astRoot != null) {
149 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
150 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
151 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
152 |     }
153 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
154 |   }

/** Gets the current scope. */

596 |   public Scope getScope() {
597 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
598 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
599 |       return scope;
600 |     }
601 |

608 |

===== M0071 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

301 |         }
302 | 
303 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
304 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
305 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
306 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
307 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
308 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
309 |             expectedConst = true;
310 |           } else {
311 |             expectedConst = false;
312 | 
313 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
314 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
315 |             if (var != null) {
316 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
317 |             }
318 | 
319 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
320 |               expectedConst = true;
321 |             } else {
322 |               expectedConst = false;
323 |             }
324 |           }
325 | 
326 |           if (expectedConst) {
327 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
328 |                 "The name %s is not annotated as constant.", name);
329 |           } else {
330 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
331 |                 "The name %s should not be annotated as constant.", name);
332 |           }
333 |         }
334 | 
335 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
336 |         if (value == null) {
337 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
338 |         } else {
339 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
340 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);
341 |         }
342 |       }
343 |     }

===== M0074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.checkRightOf(Node,Node,Predicate<Node>)

Original range: 501-511
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an expression by its root and sub-expression n, return true if there
   * the predicate is true for some expression on the right of n.
   *
   * Example:
   *
   * NotChecked(), NotChecked(), n, Checked(), Checked();
   */
Method code:
501 |   private static boolean checkRightOf(
502 |       Node n, Node expressionRoot, Predicate<Node> predicate) {
503 |     for (Node p = n; p != expressionRoot; p = p.getParent()) {
504 |       for (Node cur = p.getNext(); cur != null; cur = cur.getNext()) {
505 |         if (predicate.apply(cur)) {
506 |           return true;
507 |         }
508 |       }
509 |     }
510 |     return false;
511 |   }

===== M0080 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 990-1018
Documentation:

Method code:
990 |   private String jsString(String s, boolean useSlashV) {
991 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
992 | 
993 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
994 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
995 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
996 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
997 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
998 |       }
999 |     }
1000 | 
1001 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
1002 |     char quote;
1003 |     if (preferSingleQuotes ?
1004 |         (singleq <= doubleq) : (singleq < doubleq)) {
1005 |       // more double quotes so enclose in single quotes.
1006 |       quote = '\'';
1007 |       doublequote = "\"";
1008 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
1009 |     } else {
1010 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
1011 |       quote = '\"';
1012 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
1013 |       singlequote = "\'";
1014 |     }
1015 | 
1016 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
1017 |         outputCharsetEncoder, useSlashV, false);
1018 |   }

===== M0083 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

===== M0090 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

===== M0103 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.Candidate.canInline()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 280-411
Documentation:

Method code:
280 |     private boolean canInline() {
281 |       // Cannot inline a parameter.
282 |       if (getDefCfgNode().isFunction()) {
283 |         return false;
284 |       }
285 | 
286 |       // If one of our dependencies has been inlined, then our dependency
287 |       // graph is wrong. Re-computing it would take another CFG computation,
288 |       // so we just back off for now.
289 |       for (Var dependency : defMetadata.depends) {
290 |         if (inlinedNewDependencies.contains(dependency)) {
291 |           return false;
292 |         }
293 |       }
294 | 
295 |       getDefinition(getDefCfgNode(), null);
296 |       getNumUseInUseCfgNode(useCfgNode, null);
297 | 
298 |       // Definition was not found.
299 |       if (def == null) {
300 |         return false;
301 |       }
302 | 
303 |       // Check that the assignment isn't used as a R-Value.
304 |       // TODO(user): Certain cases we can still inline.
305 |       if (def.isAssign() && !NodeUtil.isExprAssign(def.getParent())) {
306 |         return false;
307 |       }
308 | 
309 |       // The right of the definition has side effect:
310 |       // Example, for x:
311 |       // x = readProp(b), modifyProp(b); print(x);
312 |       if (checkRightOf(def, getDefCfgNode(), SIDE_EFFECT_PREDICATE)) {
313 |         return false;
314 |       }
315 | 
316 |       // Similar check as the above but this time, all the sub-expressions
317 |       // left of the use of the variable.
318 |       // x = readProp(b); modifyProp(b), print(x);
319 |       if (checkLeftOf(use, useCfgNode, SIDE_EFFECT_PREDICATE)) {
320 |         return false;
321 |       }
322 | 
323 |       // TODO(user): Side-effect is OK sometimes. As long as there are no
324 |       // side-effect function down all paths to the use. Once we have all the
325 |       // side-effect analysis tool.
326 |       if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(def.getLastChild(), compiler)) {
327 |         return false;
328 |       }
329 | 
330 |       // TODO(user): We could inline all the uses if the expression is short.
331 | 
332 |       // Finally we have to make sure that there are no more than one use
333 |       // in the program and in the CFG node. Even when it is semantically
334 |       // correctly inlining twice increases code size.
335 |       if (numUseWithinUseCfgNode != 1) {
336 |         return false;
337 |       }
338 | 
339 |       // Make sure that the name is not within a loop
340 |       if (NodeUtil.isWithinLoop(use)) {
341 |         return false;
342 |       }
343 | 
344 | 
345 |       Collection<Node> uses = reachingUses.getUses(varName, getDefCfgNode());
346 | 
347 |       if (uses.size() != 1) {
348 |         return false;
349 |       }
350 | 
351 |       // We give up inlining stuff with R-Value that has:
352 |       // 1) GETPROP, GETELEM,
353 |       // 2) anything that creates a new object.
354 |       // 3) a direct reference to a catch expression.
355 |       // Example:
356 |       // var x = a.b.c; j.c = 1; print(x);
357 |       // Inlining print(a.b.c) is not safe consider j and be alias to a.b.
358 |       // TODO(user): We could get more accuracy by looking more in-detail
359 |       // what j is and what x is trying to into to.
360 |       // TODO(johnlenz): rework catch expression handling when we
361 |       // have lexical scope support so catch expressions don't
362 |       // need to be special cased.
363 |       if (NodeUtil.has(def.getLastChild(),
364 |           new Predicate<Node>() {
365 |               @Override
366 |               public boolean apply(Node input) {
367 |                 switch (input.getType()) {
368 |                   case Token.GETELEM:
369 |                   case Token.GETPROP:
370 |                   case Token.ARRAYLIT:
371 |                   case Token.OBJECTLIT:
372 |                   case Token.REGEXP:
373 |                   case Token.NEW:
374 |                     return true;
375 |                 }
376 |                 return false;
377 |               }
378 |           },
379 |           new Predicate<Node>() {
380 |               @Override
381 |               public boolean apply(Node input) {
382 |                 // Recurse if the node is not a function.
383 |                 return !input.isFunction();
384 |               }
385 |           })) {
386 |         return false;
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       // We can skip the side effect check along the paths of two nodes if
390 |       // they are just next to each other.
391 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(getDefCfgNode().getParent()) &&
392 |           getDefCfgNode().getNext() != useCfgNode) {
393 |         // Similar side effect check as above but this time the side effect is
394 |         // else where along the path.
395 |         // x = readProp(b); while(modifyProp(b)) {}; print(x);
396 |         CheckPathsBetweenNodes<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>
397 |           pathCheck = new CheckPathsBetweenNodes<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>(
398 |                  cfg,
399 |                  cfg.getDirectedGraphNode(getDefCfgNode()),
400 |                  cfg.getDirectedGraphNode(useCfgNode),
401 |                  SIDE_EFFECT_PREDICATE,
402 |                  Predicates.
403 |                      <DiGraphEdge<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>>alwaysTrue(),
404 |                  false);
405 |         if (pathCheck.somePathsSatisfyPredicate()) {
406 |           return false;
407 |         }
408 |       }
409 | 
410 |       return true;
411 |     }

===== M0107 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1176-1184
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1176 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1177 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1178 |       return -1;
1179 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1180 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1181 |     } else {
1182 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1183 |     }
1184 |   }

381 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

===== M0110 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 317-364
Documentation:

Method code:
317 |   @Override
318 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
319 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
320 |       return true;
321 |     }
322 | 
323 |     // Union types
324 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
325 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
326 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
327 |       return false;
328 |     }
329 | 
330 |     // record types
331 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
332 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     // Interfaces
336 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
337 |     // to the interface instance.
338 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
339 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
340 | 
341 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
342 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
343 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
344 |           return true;
345 |         }
346 |       }
347 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
348 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
349 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
350 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
351 |           return true;
352 |         }
353 |       }
354 |     }
355 | 
356 |     // other prototype based objects
357 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
358 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
359 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
360 |       // to avoid guessing.
361 |       return true;
362 |     }
363 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
364 |   }

/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
621 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
622 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
623 |         next = c.getNext();
624 |         if (c.isVar()) {
625 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
626 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
627 |           }
628 | 
629 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
630 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
631 |             c.removeChild(name);
632 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
633 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
634 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
635 |           }
636 |         }
637 |       }
638 |     }

===== M0132 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.computeEscaped(Scope,Set<Var>,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 548-578
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute set of escaped variables. When a variable is escaped in a
   * dataflow analysis, it can be reference outside of the code that we are
   * analyzing. A variable is escaped if any of the following is true:
   *
   * <p><ol>
   * <li>It is defined as the exception name in CATCH clause so it became a
   * variable local not to our definition of scope.</li>
   * <li>Exported variables as they can be needed after the script terminates.
   * </li>
   * <li>Names of named functions because in JavaScript, <i>function foo(){}</i>
   * does not kill <i>foo</i> in the dataflow.</li>
   */
Method code:
548 |   static void computeEscaped(final Scope jsScope, final Set<Var> escaped,
549 |       AbstractCompiler compiler) {
550 |     // TODO(user): Very good place to store this information somewhere.
551 |     AbstractPostOrderCallback finder = new AbstractPostOrderCallback() {
552 |       @Override
553 |       public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
554 |         if (jsScope == t.getScope() || !n.isName()
555 |             || parent.isFunction()) {
556 |           return;
557 |         }
558 |         String name = n.getString();
559 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name);
560 |         if (var != null && var.scope == jsScope) {
561 |           escaped.add(jsScope.getVar(name));
562 |         }
563 |       }
564 |     };
565 | 
566 |     NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, finder);
567 |     t.traverseAtScope(jsScope);
568 | 
569 |     // 1: Remove the exception name in CATCH which technically isn't local to
570 |     //    begin with.
571 |     for (Iterator<Var> i = jsScope.getVars(); i.hasNext();) {
572 |       Var var = i.next();
573 |       if (var.getParentNode().isCatch() ||
574 |           compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(var.getName())) {
575 |         escaped.add(var);
576 |       }
577 |     }
578 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 83-107
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
86 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);
90 |       root = result.ast;
91 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(
94 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
95 |     }
96 | 
97 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
98 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
99 |       root = IR.script();
100 |     } else {
101 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
102 |     }
103 | 
104 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
105 |     // the source file and module.
106 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
107 |   }

===== M0140 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 210-238
Documentation:

Method code:
210 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
211 |     maybeEndStatement();
212 | 
213 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
214 |     char prev = getLastChar();
215 | 
216 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
217 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
218 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
221 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
222 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
223 |       append(" ");
224 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
225 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
226 |       append(" ");
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
230 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
231 | 
232 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
233 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
234 |     // because it looks strange.
235 |     if (binOp) {
236 |       maybeCutLine();
237 |     }
238 |   }

===== M0141 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 396-421
Documentation:

Method code:
396 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
397 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
398 |     // two cases:
399 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
400 |     // and
401 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
402 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
403 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
404 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
405 |     //
406 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
407 |     //
408 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
409 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
410 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
411 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
412 |     //
413 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
414 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
415 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
416 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
417 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
418 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
419 |     }
420 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
421 |   }

===== M0142 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 490-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
490 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
491 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
492 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
493 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
494 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
495 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
496 |         String inputName = input.getName();
497 | 
498 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
499 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
500 |         // will get an error down the line.
501 |         inputs.add(input);
502 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
503 |       }
504 |     }
505 |     return inputs;
506 |   }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1252 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1253 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1254 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1255 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1256 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1257 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1258 |       } else {
1259 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1260 |       }
1261 |     }
1262 |     return builder.build();
1263 |   }

790 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
791 |     boolean valid = false;

/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
565 |   @Override
566 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
567 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
568 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
569 |     if (unknown) {
570 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
571 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
572 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
573 |         unknown = false;
574 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
575 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
576 |             unknown = true;
577 |             break;
578 |           }
579 |         }
580 |       } else {
581 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
582 |       }
583 |     }
584 |     return unknown;
585 |   }

===== M0187 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.DefinitionGatheringCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 156-260
Documentation:

Method code:
156 |     @Override
157 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
158 |       // Arguments of external functions should not count as name
159 |       // definitions.  They are placeholder names for documentation
160 |       // purposes only which are not reachable from anywhere.
161 |       if (inExterns && node.isName() && parent.isParamList()) {
162 |         return;
163 |       }
164 | 
165 |       Definition def =
166 |           DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(node, inExterns);
167 |       if (def != null) {
168 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(def.getLValue());
169 |         if (name != null) {
170 |           Node rValue = def.getRValue();
171 |           if ((rValue != null) &&
172 |               !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(rValue) &&
173 |               !rValue.isFunction()) {
174 | 
175 |             // Unhandled complex expression
176 |             Definition unknownDef =
177 |                 new UnknownDefinition(def.getLValue(), inExterns);
178 |             def = unknownDef;
179 |           }
180 | 
181 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this stub dropping code if it becomes
182 |           // illegal to have untyped stubs in the externs definitions.
183 |           if (inExterns) {
184 |             // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
185 |             //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
186 | 
187 |             List<Definition> stubsToRemove = Lists.newArrayList();
188 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
189 | 
190 |             // If there is no qualified name for this, then there will be
191 |             // no stubs to remove. This will happen if node is an object
192 |             // literal key.
193 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
194 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
195 |                 if (prevDef instanceof ExternalNameOnlyDefinition
196 |                     && !jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
197 |                   String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
198 |                   if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
199 |                     // Drop this stub, there is a real definition.
200 |                     stubsToRemove.add(prevDef);
201 |                   }
202 |                 }
203 |               }
204 | 
205 |               for (Definition prevDef : stubsToRemove) {
206 |                 nameDefinitionMultimap.remove(name, prevDef);
207 |               }
208 |             }
209 |           }
210 | 
211 |           nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, def);
212 |           definitionSiteMap.put(node,
213 |                                 new DefinitionSite(node,
214 |                                                    def,
215 |                                                    traversal.getModule(),
216 |                                                    traversal.inGlobalScope(),
217 |                                                    inExterns));
218 |         }
219 |       }
220 | 
221 |       if (inExterns && (parent != null) && parent.isExprResult()) {
222 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(node);
223 |         if (name != null) {
224 | 
225 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this code if it becomes illegal to have
226 |           // stubs in the externs definitions.
227 | 
228 |           // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
229 |           //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
230 |           // We can't just drop the stub now as it needs to be used as the
231 |           //    externs definition if no other definition is provided.
232 | 
233 |           boolean dropStub = false;
234 |           if (!jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
235 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
236 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
237 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
238 |                 String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
239 |                 if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
240 |                   dropStub = true;
241 |                   break;
242 |                 }
243 |               }
244 |             }
245 |           }
246 | 
247 |           if (!dropStub) {
248 |             // Incomplete definition
249 |             Definition definition = new ExternalNameOnlyDefinition(node);
250 |             nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, definition);
251 |             definitionSiteMap.put(node,
252 |                                   new DefinitionSite(node,
253 |                                                      definition,
254 |                                                      traversal.getModule(),
255 |                                                      traversal.inGlobalScope(),
256 |                                                      inExterns));
257 |           }
258 |         }
259 |       }
260 |     }

===== M0190 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0196 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckPathsBetweenNodes.checkSomePathsWithoutBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E>,DiGraphNode<N, E>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckPathsBetweenNodes.java
Original range: 199-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Verify that some non-looping paths from {@code a} to {@code b} pass
   * through at least one node where {@code nodePredicate} is true.
   */
Method code:
199 |   private boolean checkSomePathsWithoutBackEdges(DiGraphNode<N, E> a,
200 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> b) {
201 |     if (nodePredicate.apply(a.getValue()) &&
202 |         (inclusive || (a != start && a != end))) {
203 |       return true;
204 |     }
205 |     if (a == b) {
206 |       return false;
207 |     }
208 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> e : a.getOutEdges()) {
209 |       // Once we visited that edge once, we no longer need to
210 |       // re-visit it again.
211 |       if (e.getAnnotation() == VISITED_EDGE) {
212 |         continue;
213 |       }
214 |       e.setAnnotation(VISITED_EDGE);
215 | 
216 |       if (ignoreEdge(e)) {
217 |         continue;
218 |       }
219 |       if (e.getAnnotation() == BACK_EDGE) {
220 |         continue;
221 |       }
222 | 
223 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> next = e.getDestination();
224 |       if (checkSomePathsWithoutBackEdges(next, b)) {
225 |         return true;
226 |       }
227 |     }
228 |     return false;
229 |   }

/**
   * Returns true if a name node represents a constant variable.

   * <p>Determining whether a variable is constant has three steps:

   *     {@link CodingConvention#isConstant(String)} is annotated with an

===== M0223 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 492-505
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
492 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
493 |       Node ret;
494 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
495 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
496 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
497 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
498 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
499 |       } else {
500 |         ret = transform(n);
501 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
502 |       }
503 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
504 |       return ret;
505 |     }

===== M0224 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

/**
   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.

500 |     switch (n.getType()) {

524 |

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

===== M0259 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

===== M0260 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 564-611
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
564 |     private void extractForInitializer(
565 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
566 | 
567 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
568 |         next = c.getNext();
569 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
570 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
571 |         switch (c.getType()) {
572 |           case Token.LABEL:
573 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
574 |             break;
575 |           case Token.FOR:
576 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
577 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
578 |               if (first.isVar()) {
579 |                 // Transform:
580 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
581 |                 // to:
582 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
583 |                 Node newStatement = first;
584 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
585 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
586 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
587 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
588 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
589 |               }
590 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
591 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
592 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
593 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
594 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
595 | 
596 |               Node newStatement;
597 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
598 |               // but are handled differently.
599 |               if (init.isVar()) {
600 |                 newStatement = init;
601 |               } else {
602 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
603 |               }
604 | 
605 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
606 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
607 |             }
608 |             break;
609 |         }
610 |       }
611 |     }

===== M0261 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

===== M0266 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0283 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.Candidate.getNumUseInUseCfgNode(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 473-490
Documentation:
/**
     * Computes the number of uses of the variable varName and store it in
     * numUseWithinUseCfgNode.
     */
Method code:
473 |     private void getNumUseInUseCfgNode(Node n, Node parant) {
474 | 
475 |       AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback gatherCb =
476 |           new AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback() {
477 | 
478 |         @Override
479 |         public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
480 |           if (n.isName() && n.getString().equals(varName) &&
481 |               // do not count in if it is left child of an assignment operator
482 |               !(parent.isAssign() &&
483 |                (parent.getFirstChild() == n))) {
484 |             numUseWithinUseCfgNode++;
485 |           }
486 |         }
487 |       };
488 | 
489 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, n, gatherCb);
490 |     }
