===== M0285 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 103-756
Documentation:

Method code:
103 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
104 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
105 |       return;
106 |     }
107 | 
108 |     int type = n.getType();
109 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
110 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
111 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
112 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
113 | 
114 |     // Handle all binary operators
115 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(
117 |           childCount == 2,
118 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
119 |           opstr, childCount);
120 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
121 | 
122 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
123 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
124 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
125 | 
126 |       // Handle associativity.
127 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
128 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
129 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
130 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
131 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
132 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
133 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
134 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
135 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
136 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
137 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
138 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
139 |       } else {
140 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
141 |       }
142 |       return;
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
146 | 
147 |     switch (type) {
148 |       case Token.TRY: {
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
150 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
152 | 
153 |         add("try");
154 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
155 | 
156 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
157 |         // isn't a catch block
158 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
159 |         if (catchblock != null) {
160 |           add(catchblock);
161 |         }
162 | 
163 |         if (childCount == 3) {
164 |           add("finally");
165 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
166 |         }
167 |         break;
168 |       }
169 | 
170 |       case Token.CATCH:
171 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
172 |         add("catch(");
173 |         add(first);
174 |         add(")");
175 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.THROW:
179 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
180 |         add("throw");
181 |         add(first);
182 | 
183 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
184 |         // parse this.
185 |         cc.endStatement(true);
186 |         break;
187 | 
188 |       case Token.RETURN:
189 |         add("return");
190 |         if (childCount == 1) {
191 |           add(first);
192 |         } else {
193 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
194 |         }
195 |         cc.endStatement();
196 |         break;
197 | 
198 |       case Token.VAR:
199 |         if (first != null) {
200 |           add("var ");
201 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
202 |         }
203 |         break;
204 | 
205 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
206 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
207 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.NAME:
211 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
212 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
213 |         } else {
214 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
215 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
216 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
217 |           if (first.isComma()) {
218 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
219 |           } else {
220 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
221 |             // precedence.
222 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
223 |           }
224 |         }
225 |         break;
226 | 
227 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
228 |         add("[");
229 |         addArrayList(first);
230 |         add("]");
231 |         break;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
234 |         add("(");
235 |         addList(first);
236 |         add(")");
237 |         break;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.COMMA:
240 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
241 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
242 |         break;
243 | 
244 |       case Token.NUMBER:
245 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
246 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
247 |         break;
248 | 
249 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
250 |       case Token.VOID:
251 |       case Token.NOT:
252 |       case Token.BITNOT:
253 |       case Token.POS: {
254 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
255 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
256 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
257 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
258 |         break;
259 |       }
260 | 
261 |       case Token.NEG: {
262 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
263 | 
264 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
265 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
266 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
267 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
268 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
269 |         } else {
270 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
271 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         break;
275 |       }
276 | 
277 |       case Token.HOOK: {
278 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
279 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
280 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
281 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
282 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
283 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
284 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
285 |         break;
286 |       }
287 | 
288 |       case Token.REGEXP:
289 |         if (!first.isString() ||
290 |             !last.isString()) {
291 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
292 |         }
293 | 
294 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
295 | 
296 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
297 |         if (childCount == 2) {
298 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
299 |         } else {
300 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
301 |           add(regexp);
302 |         }
303 |         break;
304 | 
305 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
306 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
307 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
308 |         }
309 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
310 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
311 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
312 |           add("(");
313 |         }
314 | 
315 |         add("function");
316 |         add(first);
317 | 
318 |         add(first.getNext());
319 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
320 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
321 | 
322 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
323 |           add(")");
324 |         }
325 |         break;
326 | 
327 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
328 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
329 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
330 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
331 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
332 | 
333 |         // Get methods are unnamed
334 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
335 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
336 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
337 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
338 |           add("get ");
339 |         } else {
340 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
341 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
342 |           add("set ");
343 |         }
344 | 
345 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
346 |         String name = n.getString();
347 |         Node fn = first;
348 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
349 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
350 | 
351 |         // Add the property name.
352 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
353 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
354 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
355 |             // Unicode escaped.
356 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
357 |           add(name);
358 |         } else {
359 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
360 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
361 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
362 |             cc.addNumber(d);
363 |           } else {
364 |             addJsString(n);
365 |           }
366 |         }
367 | 
368 |         add(parameters);
369 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
370 |         break;
371 | 
372 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
373 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
374 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
375 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
376 |         }
377 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
378 |         if (preserveBlock) {
379 |           cc.beginBlock();
380 |         }
381 | 
382 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
383 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
384 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
385 |                 !preserveBlock &&
386 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
387 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
388 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
389 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
390 | 
391 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
392 |           if (c.isVar()) {
393 |             cc.endStatement();
394 |           }
395 | 
396 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
397 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
398 |           }
399 | 
400 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
401 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
402 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
403 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
404 |           }
405 |         }
406 |         if (preserveBlock) {
407 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
408 |         }
409 |         break;
410 |       }
411 | 
412 |       case Token.FOR:
413 |         if (childCount == 4) {
414 |           add("for(");
415 |           if (first.isVar()) {
416 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
417 |           } else {
418 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
419 |           }
420 |           add(";");
421 |           add(first.getNext());
422 |           add(";");
423 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
424 |           add(")");
425 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
426 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
427 |         } else {
428 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
429 |           add("for(");
430 |           add(first);
431 |           add("in");
432 |           add(first.getNext());
433 |           add(")");
434 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
435 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
436 |         }
437 |         break;
438 | 
439 |       case Token.DO:
440 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
441 |         add("do");
442 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
443 |         add("while(");
444 |         add(last);
445 |         add(")");
446 |         cc.endStatement();
447 |         break;
448 | 
449 |       case Token.WHILE:
450 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
451 |         add("while(");
452 |         add(first);
453 |         add(")");
454 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
455 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
456 |         break;
457 | 
458 |       case Token.EMPTY:
459 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
460 |         break;
461 | 
462 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
463 |         Preconditions.checkState(
464 |             childCount == 2,
465 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
466 |         Preconditions.checkState(
467 |             last.isString(),
468 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
469 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
470 |         if (needsParens) {
471 |           add("(");
472 |         }
473 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
474 |         if (needsParens) {
475 |           add(")");
476 |         }
477 |         add(".");
478 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
479 |         break;
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       case Token.GETELEM:
483 |         Preconditions.checkState(
484 |             childCount == 2,
485 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
486 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
487 |         add("[");
488 |         add(first.getNext());
489 |         add("]");
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.WITH:
493 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
494 |         add("with(");
495 |         add(first);
496 |         add(")");
497 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
498 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
499 |         break;
500 | 
501 |       case Token.INC:
502 |       case Token.DEC: {
503 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
504 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
505 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
506 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
507 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
508 |         if (postProp != 0) {
509 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
510 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
511 |         } else {
512 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
513 |           add(first);
514 |         }
515 |         break;
516 |       }
517 | 
518 |       case Token.CALL:
519 |         // We have two special cases here:
520 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
521 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
522 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
523 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
524 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
525 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
526 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
527 |         // that must be preserved.
528 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
529 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
530 |           add("(0,");
531 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
532 |           add(")");
533 |         } else {
534 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
535 |         }
536 |         add("(");
537 |         addList(first.getNext());
538 |         add(")");
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.IF:
542 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
543 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
544 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
545 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
546 |           cc.beginBlock();
547 |         }
548 | 
549 |         add("if(");
550 |         add(first);
551 |         add(")");
552 | 
553 |         if (hasElse) {
554 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
555 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
556 |           add("else");
557 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
558 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
559 |         } else {
560 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
561 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
562 |         }
563 | 
564 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
565 |           cc.endBlock();
566 |         }
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.NULL:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
571 |         cc.addConstant("null");
572 |         break;
573 | 
574 |       case Token.THIS:
575 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
576 |         add("this");
577 |         break;
578 | 
579 |       case Token.FALSE:
580 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
581 |         cc.addConstant("false");
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.TRUE:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         cc.addConstant("true");
587 |         break;
588 | 
589 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
590 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
591 |         add("continue");
592 |         if (childCount == 1) {
593 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
594 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
595 |           }
596 |           add(" ");
597 |           add(first);
598 |         }
599 |         cc.endStatement();
600 |         break;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
603 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
604 |         add("debugger");
605 |         cc.endStatement();
606 |         break;
607 | 
608 |       case Token.BREAK:
609 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
610 |         add("break");
611 |         if (childCount == 1) {
612 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
613 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
614 |           }
615 |           add(" ");
616 |           add(first);
617 |         }
618 |         cc.endStatement();
619 |         break;
620 | 
621 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
622 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
623 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
624 |         cc.endStatement();
625 |         break;
626 | 
627 |       case Token.NEW:
628 |         add("new ");
629 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
630 | 
631 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
632 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
633 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
634 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
635 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
636 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
637 |         }
638 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
639 | 
640 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
641 |         Node next = first.getNext();
642 |         if (next != null) {
643 |           add("(");
644 |           addList(next);
645 |           add(")");
646 |         }
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
650 |         Preconditions.checkState(
651 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
652 |         addJsString(n);
653 |         break;
654 | 
655 |       case Token.STRING:
656 |         Preconditions.checkState(
657 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
658 |         addJsString(n);
659 |         break;
660 | 
661 |       case Token.DELPROP:
662 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
663 |         add("delete ");
664 |         add(first);
665 |         break;
666 | 
667 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
668 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
669 |         if (needsParens) {
670 |           add("(");
671 |         }
672 |         add("{");
673 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
674 |           if (c != first) {
675 |             cc.listSeparator();
676 |           }
677 | 
678 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
679 |             add(c);
680 |           } else {
681 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
682 |             String key = c.getString();
683 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
684 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
685 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
686 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
687 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
688 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
689 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
690 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
691 |               add(key);
692 |             } else {
693 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
694 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
695 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
696 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
697 |               } else {
698 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
699 |               }
700 |             }
701 |             add(":");
702 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
703 |           }
704 |         }
705 |         add("}");
706 |         if (needsParens) {
707 |           add(")");
708 |         }
709 |         break;
710 |       }
711 | 
712 |       case Token.SWITCH:
713 |         add("switch(");
714 |         add(first);
715 |         add(")");
716 |         cc.beginBlock();
717 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
718 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
723 |         add("case ");
724 |         add(first);
725 |         addCaseBody(last);
726 |         break;
727 | 
728 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
729 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
730 |         add("default");
731 |         addCaseBody(first);
732 |         break;
733 | 
734 |       case Token.LABEL:
735 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
736 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
737 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
738 |         }
739 |         add(first);
740 |         add(":");
741 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
742 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
743 |         break;
744 | 
745 |       case Token.CAST:
746 |         add("(");
747 |         add(first);
748 |         add(")");
749 |         break;
750 | 
751 |       default:
752 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
753 |     }
754 | 
755 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
756 |   }

===== M0286 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 286-324
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the new flow state at a given node's entry by merging the
   * output (input) lattice of the node's predecessor (successor).
   *
   * @param node Node to compute new join.
   */
Method code:
286 |   protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
287 |     FlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
288 |     if (isForward()) {
289 |       if (cfg.getEntry() == node) {
290 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
291 |       } else {
292 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> inNodes = cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(node);
293 |         if (inNodes.size() == 1) {
294 |           FlowState<L> inNodeState = inNodes.get(0).getAnnotation();
295 |           state.setIn(inNodeState.getOut());
296 |         } else if (inNodes.size() > 1) {
297 |           List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(inNodes.size());
298 |           for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> currentNode : inNodes) {
299 |             FlowState<L> currentNodeState = currentNode.getAnnotation();
300 |             values.add(currentNodeState.getOut());
301 |           }
302 |           state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |     } else {
306 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> inNodes = cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(node);
307 |       if (inNodes.size() == 1) {
308 |         DiGraphNode<N, Branch> inNode = inNodes.get(0);
309 |         if (inNode == cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
310 |           state.setOut(createEntryLattice());
311 |         } else {
312 |           FlowState<L> inNodeState = inNode.getAnnotation();
313 |           state.setOut(inNodeState.getIn());
314 |         }
315 |       } else if (inNodes.size() > 1) {
316 |         List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(inNodes.size());
317 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> currentNode : inNodes) {
318 |           FlowState<L> currentNodeState = currentNode.getAnnotation();
319 |           values.add(currentNodeState.getIn());
320 |         }
321 |         state.setOut(joinOp.apply(values));
322 |       }
323 |     }
324 |   }

211 |         public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

===== M0314 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 553-568
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
553 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
554 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
555 |       Set<String> directives = null;
556 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
557 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
558 |         if (directives == null) {
559 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
560 |         } else {
561 |           directives.add(directive);
562 |         }
563 |       }
564 | 
565 |       if (directives != null) {
566 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
567 |       }
568 |     }

===== M0319 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 182-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
182 |   boolean parse() {
183 |     int lineno;
184 |     int charno;
185 | 
186 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
187 |     JSTypeExpression type;
188 | 
189 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
190 |     skipEOLs();
191 | 
192 |     JsDocToken token = next();
193 | 
194 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
195 | 
196 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
197 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
198 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
199 |       token = blockInfo.token;
200 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
201 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
202 |       }
203 |     } else {
204 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
205 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
206 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
207 |         // what it was.
208 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
213 |     retry: for (;;) {
214 |       switch (token) {
215 |         case ANNOTATION:
216 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
217 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
218 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
219 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
220 | 
221 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
222 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
223 |             if (annotation == null) {
224 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
225 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |             } else {
227 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
228 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
229 | 
230 |               switch (annotation) {
231 |                 case AUTHOR:
232 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
233 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
234 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
235 | 
236 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
237 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
238 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
239 |                     } else {
240 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
241 |                     }
242 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
243 |                   } else {
244 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
245 |                   }
246 |                   continue retry;
247 | 
248 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
249 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
250 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
251 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
252 |                   }
253 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
254 |                   continue retry;
255 | 
256 |                 case STRUCT:
257 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordStruct()) {
258 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
259 |                                           stream.getLineno(),
260 |                                           stream.getCharno());
261 |                   }
262 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
263 |                   continue retry;
264 | 
265 |                 case DICT:
266 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDict()) {
267 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
268 |                                           stream.getLineno(),
269 |                                           stream.getCharno());
270 |                   }
271 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
272 |                   continue retry;
273 | 
274 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
275 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
276 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
277 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
278 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
279 |                     } else {
280 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
281 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
282 |                     }
283 |                   }
284 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
285 |                   continue retry;
286 | 
287 |                 case DEPRECATED:
288 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
289 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
290 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
291 |                   }
292 | 
293 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
294 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
295 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
296 | 
297 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
298 | 
299 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
300 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
301 |                   }
302 | 
303 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
304 |                   continue retry;
305 | 
306 |                 case INTERFACE:

1356 |         }

1361 |         }

===== M0323 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.Candidate.inlineVariable()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 416-437
Documentation:
/**
     * Actual transformation.
     */
Method code:
416 |     private void inlineVariable() {
417 |       Node defParent = def.getParent();
418 |       Node useParent = use.getParent();
419 |       if (def.isAssign()) {
420 |         Node rhs = def.getLastChild();
421 |         rhs.detachFromParent();
422 |         // Oh yes! I have grandparent to remove this.
423 |         Preconditions.checkState(defParent.isExprResult());
424 |         while (defParent.getParent().isLabel()) {
425 |           defParent = defParent.getParent();
426 |         }
427 |         defParent.detachFromParent();
428 |         useParent.replaceChild(use, rhs);
429 |       } else if (defParent.isVar()) {
430 |         Node rhs = def.getLastChild();
431 |         def.removeChild(rhs);
432 |         useParent.replaceChild(use, rhs);
433 |       } else {
434 |         Preconditions.checkState(false, "No other definitions can be inlined.");
435 |       }
436 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
437 |     }

===== M0324 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 796-810
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthrough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
796 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
797 |     switch (n.getType()) {
798 |       case Token.DO:
799 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
800 |       case Token.FOR:
801 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
802 |           return n.getFirstChild().getNext();
803 |         }
804 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
805 |       case Token.LABEL:
806 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
807 |       default:
808 |         return n;
809 |     }
810 |   }

===== M0327 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.JSType(JSTypeRegistry,ImmutableList<String>,ImmutableList<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 114-144
Documentation:

Method code:
114 |   JSType(JSTypeRegistry registry, ImmutableList<String> templateKeys,
115 |       ImmutableList<JSType> templatizedTypes) {
116 |     this.registry = registry;
117 | 
118 |     // Do sanity checking on the specified keys and templatized types.
119 |     int keysLength = templateKeys == null ? 0 : templateKeys.size();
120 |     int typesLength = templatizedTypes == null ? 0 : templatizedTypes.size();
121 |     if (typesLength > keysLength) {
122 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(
123 |           "Cannot have more templatized types than template keys");
124 |     } else if (typesLength < keysLength) {
125 |       // If there are fewer templatized types than keys, extend the templatized
126 |       // types list to match the number of keys, using UNKNOWN_TYPE for the
127 |       // unspecified types.
128 |       ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> builder = ImmutableList.builder();
129 |       if (typesLength > 0) {
130 |         builder.addAll(templatizedTypes);
131 |       }
132 |       for (int i = 0; i < keysLength - typesLength; i++) {
133 |         builder.add(registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE));
134 |       }
135 |       templatizedTypes = builder.build();
136 |     } else if (keysLength == 0 && typesLength == 0) {
137 |       // Ensure that both lists are non-null.
138 |       templateKeys = ImmutableList.of();
139 |       templatizedTypes = ImmutableList.of();
140 |     }
141 | 
142 |     this.templateKeys = templateKeys;
143 |     this.templatizedTypes = templatizedTypes;
144 |   }

===== M0334 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.getSimplifiedName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 135-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a name from a node.  In the case of GETPROP nodes,
   * replace the namespace or object expression with "this" for
   * simplicity and correctness at the expense of inefficiencies due
   * to higher chances of name collisions.
   *
   * TODO(user) revisit.  it would be helpful to at least use fully
   * qualified names in the case of namespaces.  Might not matter as
   * much if this pass runs after "collapsing properties".
   */
Method code:
135 |   private static String getSimplifiedName(Node node) {
136 |     if (node.isName()) {
137 |       String name = node.getString();
138 |       if (name != null && !name.isEmpty()) {
139 |         return name;
140 |       } else {
141 |         return null;
142 |       }
143 |     } else if (node.isGetProp()) {
144 |       return "this." + node.getLastChild().getString();
145 |     }
146 |     return null;
147 |   }

===== M0357 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.GatherCandiates.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 197-244
Documentation:

Method code:
197 |     @Override
198 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
199 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> graphNode = cfg.getDirectedGraphNode(n);
200 |       if (graphNode == null) {
201 |         // Not a CFG node.
202 |         return;
203 |       }
204 |       FlowState<MustDef> state = graphNode.getAnnotation();
205 |       final MustDef defs = state.getIn();
206 |       final Node cfgNode = n;
207 |       AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback gatherCb =
208 |           new AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback() {
209 | 
210 |         @Override
211 |         public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
212 |           if (n.isName()) {
213 | 
214 |             // n.getParent() isn't null. This just the case where n is the root
215 |             // node that gatherCb started at.
216 |             if (parent == null) {
217 |               return;
218 |             }
219 | 
220 |             // Make sure that the name node is purely a read.
221 |             if ((NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
222 |                 || parent.isVar() || parent.isInc() || parent.isDec() ||
223 |                 parent.isParamList() || parent.isCatch()) {
224 |               return;
225 |             }
226 | 
227 |             String name = n.getString();
228 |             if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(name)) {
229 |               return;
230 |             }
231 | 
232 |             Definition def = reachingDef.getDef(name, cfgNode);
233 |             // TODO(nicksantos): We need to add some notion of @const outer
234 |             // scope vars. We can inline those just fine.
235 |             if (def != null &&
236 |                 !reachingDef.dependsOnOuterScopeVars(def)) {
237 |               candidates.add(new Candidate(name, def, n, cfgNode));
238 |             }
239 |           }
240 |         }
241 |       };
242 | 
243 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, cfgNode, gatherCb);
244 |     }

===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MustBeReachingVariableDef.computeMustDef(Node,Node,MustDef,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MustBeReachingVariableDef.java
Original range: 225-318
Documentation:
/**
   * @param n The node in question.
   * @param cfgNode The node to add
   * @param conditional true if the definition is not always executed.
   */
Method code:
225 |   private void computeMustDef(
226 |       Node n, Node cfgNode, MustDef output, boolean conditional) {
227 |     switch (n.getType()) {
228 | 
229 |       case Token.BLOCK:
230 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
231 |         return;
232 | 
233 |       case Token.WHILE:
234 |       case Token.DO:
235 |       case Token.IF:
236 |         computeMustDef(
237 |             NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
238 |         return;
239 | 
240 |       case Token.FOR:
241 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
242 |           computeMustDef(
243 |               NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
244 |         } else {
245 |           // for(x in y) {...}
246 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
247 |           Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
248 |           if (lhs.isVar()) {
249 |             lhs = lhs.getLastChild(); // for(var x in y) {...}
250 |           }
251 |           if (lhs.isName()) {
252 |             addToDefIfLocal(lhs.getString(), cfgNode, rhs, output);
253 |           }
254 |         }
255 |         return;
256 | 
257 |       case Token.AND:
258 |       case Token.OR:
259 |         computeMustDef(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
260 |         computeMustDef(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
261 |         return;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.HOOK:
264 |         computeMustDef(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
265 |         computeMustDef(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), cfgNode, output, true);
266 |         computeMustDef(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
267 |         return;
268 | 
269 |       case Token.VAR:
270 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
271 |           if (c.hasChildren()) {
272 |             computeMustDef(c.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
273 |             addToDefIfLocal(c.getString(), conditional ? null : cfgNode,
274 |                 c.getFirstChild(), output);
275 |           }
276 |         }
277 |         return;
278 | 
279 |       default:
280 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n)) {
281 |           if (n.getFirstChild().isName()) {
282 |             Node name = n.getFirstChild();
283 |             computeMustDef(name.getNext(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
284 |             addToDefIfLocal(name.getString(), conditional ? null : cfgNode,
285 |               n.getLastChild(), output);
286 |             return;
287 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isGet(n.getFirstChild())) {
288 |             // Treat all assignments to arguments as redefining the
289 |             // parameters itself.
290 |             Node obj = n.getFirstChild().getFirstChild();
291 |             if (obj.isName() && "arguments".equals(obj.getString())) {
292 |               // TODO(user): More accuracy can be introduced
293 |               // i.e. We know exactly what arguments[x] is if x is a constant
294 |               // number.
295 |               escapeParameters(output);
296 |             }
297 |           }
298 |         }
299 | 
300 |         if (n.isName() && "arguments".equals(n.getString())) {
301 |           escapeParameters(output);
302 |         }
303 | 
304 |         // DEC and INC actually defines the variable.
305 |         if (n.isDec() || n.isInc()) {
306 |           Node target = n.getFirstChild();
307 |           if (target.isName()) {
308 |             addToDefIfLocal(target.getString(),
309 |                 conditional ? null : cfgNode, null, output);
310 |             return;
311 |           }
312 |         }
313 | 
314 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
315 |           computeMustDef(c, cfgNode, output, conditional);
316 |         }
317 |     }
318 |   }

===== M0380 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 513-541
Documentation:

Method code:
513 |     @Override
514 |     void endFile() {
515 |       super.endFile();
516 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
517 |         return;
518 |       }
519 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
520 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
521 |         append(";");
522 |         startNewLine();
523 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
524 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
525 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
526 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
527 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
528 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
529 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
530 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
531 |         lineIndex--;
532 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
533 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
534 |         append(";");
535 |         startNewLine();
536 |       } else {
537 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
538 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
539 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
540 |       }
541 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1173-1248
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1173 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1174 |     switch (type) {
1175 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1177 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1178 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1179 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1180 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1182 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1183 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1184 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1185 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1186 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1187 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1188 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1189 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1190 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1191 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1192 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1193 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1194 |       case Token.EQ:
1195 |       case Token.NE:
1196 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1197 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1198 |       case Token.LT:
1199 |       case Token.GT:
1200 |       case Token.LE:
1201 |       case Token.GE:
1202 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1203 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1204 |       case Token.LSH:
1205 |       case Token.RSH:
1206 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1207 |       case Token.SUB:
1208 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1209 |       case Token.MUL:
1210 |       case Token.MOD:
1211 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1212 |       case Token.INC:
1213 |       case Token.DEC:
1214 |       case Token.NEW:
1215 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1216 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1217 |       case Token.VOID:
1218 |       case Token.NOT:
1219 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1220 |       case Token.POS:
1221 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1222 | 
1223 |       case Token.CALL:
1224 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1225 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1226 |       // Data values
1227 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1228 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1229 |       case Token.FALSE:
1230 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1231 |       case Token.NAME:
1232 |       case Token.NULL:
1233 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1234 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1235 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1236 |       case Token.STRING:
1237 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
1238 |       case Token.THIS:
1239 |       case Token.TRUE:
1240 |         return 15;
1241 |       case Token.CAST:
1242 |         return 16;
1243 | 
1244 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1245 |                                Token.name(type) +
1246 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1247 |     }
1248 |   }

===== M0390 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 240-276
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   void addNumber(double x) {
241 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
242 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
243 |     char prev = getLastChar();
244 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
245 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
246 |       add(" ");
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (negativeZero) {
250 |       addConstant("-0");
251 |     } else if ((long) x == x) {
252 |       long value = (long) x;
253 |       long mantissa = value;
254 |       int exp = 0;
255 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
256 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
257 |           mantissa /= 10;
258 |           exp++;
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 |       if (exp > 2) {
262 |         addConstant(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
263 |       } else {
264 |         long valueAbs = Math.abs(value);
265 |         if (Long.toHexString(valueAbs).length() + 2 <
266 |             Long.toString(valueAbs).length()) {
267 |           addConstant((value < 0 ? "-" : "") + "0x" +
268 |               Long.toHexString(valueAbs));
269 |         } else {
270 |           addConstant(Long.toString(value));
271 |         }
272 |       }
273 |     } else {
274 |       addConstant(String.valueOf(x).replace(".0E", "E"));
275 |     }
276 |   }

===== M0391 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.isConnectedInDirection(N,Predicate<E>,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 203-215
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
208 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {
210 |         return true;
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;
215 |   }

===== M0403 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 288-311
Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

704 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {

===== M0430 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.unrollBinaryOperator(Node,int,String,Context,Context,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 765-781
Documentation:
/**
   * We could use addList recursively here, but sometimes we produce
   * very deeply nested operators and run out of stack space, so we
   * just unroll the recursion when possible.
   *
   * We assume nodes are left-recursive.
   */
Method code:
765 |   private void unrollBinaryOperator(
766 |       Node n, int op, String opStr, Context context,
767 |       Context rhsContext, int leftPrecedence, int rightPrecedence) {
768 |     Node firstNonOperator = n.getFirstChild();
769 |     while (firstNonOperator.getType() == op) {
770 |       firstNonOperator = firstNonOperator.getFirstChild();
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     addExpr(firstNonOperator, leftPrecedence, context);
774 | 
775 |     Node current = firstNonOperator;
776 |     do {
777 |       current = current.getParent();
778 |       cc.addOp(opStr, true);
779 |       addExpr(current.getFirstChild().getNext(), rightPrecedence, rhsContext);
780 |     } while (current != n);
781 |   }

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.
   *
   * @see #analyze()
   */

===== M0434 =====

/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
489 |     @Override
490 |     void maybeCutLine() {
491 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
492 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
493 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
494 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
495 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
496 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
497 |           prevCutPosition = position;
498 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
499 |           lineIndex++;
500 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
501 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
502 |         } else {
503 |           startNewLine();
504 |         }
505 |       }
506 |     }

===== M0435 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 159-205
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
159 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
160 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
161 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
162 |       case Token.BLOCK:
163 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
164 |       case Token.TRY:
165 |         return true;
166 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
167 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
168 |         // bleeds into the local scope and parameters are assigned
169 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
170 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
171 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
172 |         // control is going into the function's body, represented by the second
173 |         // child.
174 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
175 |       case Token.WHILE:
176 |       case Token.DO:
177 |       case Token.IF:
178 |         // These control structures are represented by a node that holds the
179 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
180 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.FOR:
183 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structures in that
184 |         // it has an initialization and an increment statement. Those
185 |         // two statements have corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
186 |         // The FOR node only represents the condition check for each iteration.
187 |         // That way the following:
188 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
189 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
190 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
191 |           // TODO(user): Investigate how we should handle the case where
192 |           // we have a very complex expression inside the FOR-IN header.
193 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
194 |         } else {
195 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
196 |         }
197 |       case Token.SWITCH:
198 |       case Token.CASE:
199 |       case Token.CATCH:
200 |       case Token.WITH:
201 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
202 |       default:
203 |         return false;
204 |     }
205 |   }

===== M0439 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1332-1334
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1332 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1333 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1334 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MustBeReachingVariableDef.addToDefIfLocal(String,Node,Node,MustDef)

Documentation:
/**
   * Set the variable lattice for the given name to the node value in the def
   * lattice. Do nothing if the variable name is one of the escaped variable.
   *
   * @param node The CFG node where the definition should be record to.
   *     {@code null} if this is a conditional define.
   */
Method code:
327 |   private void addToDefIfLocal( String name, @Nullable Node node,
328 |       @Nullable Node rValue, MustDef def) {
329 |     Var var = jsScope.getVar(name);
330 | 
331 |     // var might be null because the variable might be defined in the extern
332 |     // that we might not traverse.
333 |     if (var == null || var.scope != jsScope) {
334 |       return;
335 |     }
336 | 
337 |     for (Var other : def.reachingDef.keySet()) {
338 |       Definition otherDef = def.reachingDef.get(other);
339 |       if (otherDef == null) {
340 |         continue;
341 |       }
342 |       if (otherDef.depends.contains(var)) {
343 |         def.reachingDef.put(other, null);
344 |       }
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     if (!escaped.contains(var)) {
348 |       if (node == null) {
349 |         def.reachingDef.put(var, null);
350 |       } else {
351 |         Definition definition = new Definition(node);
352 |         if (rValue != null) {
353 |           computeDependence(definition, rValue);
354 |         }
355 |         def.reachingDef.put(var, definition);
356 |       }
357 |     }
358 |   }

509 |   private void validateTry(Node n) {
510 |     validateNodeType(Token.TRY, n);
511 |     validateMinimumChildCount(n, 2);
512 |     validateMaximumChildCount(n, 3);
513 |     validateBlock(n.getFirstChild());
514 | 
515 |     boolean seenCatchOrFinally = false;
516 | 
517 |     // Validate catch
518 |     Node catches = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
519 |     validateNodeType(Token.BLOCK, catches);
520 |     validateMaximumChildCount(catches, 1);

523 |       seenCatchOrFinally = true;
524 |     }
525 |

527 |     if (n.getChildCount() == 3) {
528 |       validateBlock(n.getLastChild());
529 |       seenCatchOrFinally = true;
530 |     }
531 | 
532 |     if (!seenCatchOrFinally) {
533 |       violation("Missing catch or finally for try statement.", n);
534 |     }
535 |   }

===== M0459 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1340-1376
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1340 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1341 |     // unknown
1342 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1343 |       return true;
1344 |     }
1345 |     // all type
1346 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1347 |       return true;
1348 |     }
1349 |     // equality
1350 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1351 |       return true;
1352 |     }
1353 |     // unions
1354 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1355 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1356 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1357 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1358 |           return true;
1359 |         }
1360 |       }
1361 |       return false;
1362 |     }
1363 | 
1364 |     // parameterized types.
1365 |     if (thisType.isParameterizedType()) {
1366 |       return thisType.toMaybeParameterizedType().isParameterizeSubtypeOf(
1367 |           thatType);
1368 |     }
1369 | 
1370 |     // proxy types
1371 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1372 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1373 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1374 |     }
1375 |     return false;
1376 |   }

===== M0460 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 639-666
Documentation:

Method code:
639 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
640 |     Node lastJump = null;
641 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
642 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
643 |       if (curHandler.isFunction()) {
644 |         break;
645 |       }
646 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
647 |         if (lastJump == null) {
648 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
649 |         } else {
650 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
651 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
652 |         }
653 |         lastJump = curHandler;
654 |       }
655 |     }
656 | 
657 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
658 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
659 |     }
660 | 
661 |     if (lastJump == null) {
662 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
663 |     } else {
664 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
665 |     }
666 |   }

===== M0471 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 624-636
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
624 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
625 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
626 |     if (typeSet == null) {
627 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
628 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
632 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
633 | 
634 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
635 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
636 |   }

===== M0485 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1282-1404
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1282 |   Node parseInputs() {
1283 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1284 | 
1285 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1286 |     // individual file parse trees.
1287 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1288 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1289 |     }
1290 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1291 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1292 |     }
1293 | 
1294 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1295 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1296 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1297 | 
1298 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1299 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1300 | 
1301 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1302 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1303 | 
1304 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1305 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1306 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1307 |     }
1308 | 
1309 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1310 | 
1311 |     try {
1312 |       // Parse externs sources.
1313 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1314 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1315 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1316 |           return null;
1317 |         }
1318 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1319 |       }
1320 | 
1321 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1322 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1323 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1324 |       }
1325 | 
1326 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1327 | 
1328 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1329 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1330 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1331 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1332 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1333 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1334 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1335 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1336 |           }
1337 |         }
1338 | 
1339 |         try {
1340 |           inputs =
1341 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1342 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1343 |           staleInputs = true;
1344 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1345 |           report(JSError.make(
1346 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1347 | 
1348 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1349 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1350 |             return null;
1351 |           }
1352 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1353 |           report(JSError.make(
1354 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1355 | 
1356 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1357 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1358 |             return null;
1359 |           }
1360 |         }
1361 |       }
1362 | 
1363 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1364 | 
1365 |       if (staleInputs) {
1366 |         repartitionInputs();
1367 |       }
1368 | 
1369 |       // Build the AST.
1370 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1371 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1372 |         if (n == null) {
1373 |           continue;
1374 |         }
1375 | 
1376 |         if (devMode) {
1377 |           runSanityCheck();
1378 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1379 |             return null;
1380 |           }
1381 |         }
1382 | 
1383 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1384 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1385 | 
1386 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1387 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1388 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1389 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1390 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1391 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1392 |         }
1393 | 
1394 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1395 |       }
1396 | 
1397 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1398 |         return null;
1399 |       }
1400 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1401 |     } finally {
1402 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1403 |     }
1404 |   }

===== M0486 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 785-798
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
785 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
786 |     if (item == null) {
787 |       return null;
788 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
789 |       return item.getNext();
790 |     } else {
791 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
792 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
793 |         return item.chain(result);
794 |       } else {
795 |         return item;
796 |       }
797 |     }
798 |   }

===== M0499 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(CodingConvention,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2816-2827
Documentation:
/** Whether the given name is constant by coding convention. */
Method code:
2816 |   static boolean isConstantByConvention(
2817 |       CodingConvention convention, Node node, Node parent) {
2818 |     String name = node.getString();
2819 |     if (parent.isGetProp() &&
2820 |         node == parent.getLastChild()) {
2821 |       return convention.isConstantKey(name);
2822 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(node, parent)) {
2823 |       return convention.isConstantKey(name);
2824 |     } else {
2825 |       return convention.isConstant(name);
2826 |     }
2827 |   }

===== M0510 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 120-159
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   @Override
121 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
122 |     new NodeTraversal(
123 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
124 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
125 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
126 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
127 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
128 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
129 |     }
130 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
131 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
132 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
133 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our current Scope logic
134 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
135 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
136 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
137 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
138 |     //   function f() {
139 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
140 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
141 |     //   }
142 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
143 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a separate scope for catch nodes.
144 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
145 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
146 |         .process(externs, root);
147 | 
148 |     FindExposeAnnotations findExposeAnnotations = new FindExposeAnnotations();
149 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, findExposeAnnotations);
150 |     if (!findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties.isEmpty()) {
151 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
152 |           new RewriteExposedProperties(
153 |               findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties));
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
157 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
158 |     }
159 |   }

===== M0522 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.doStatementNormalizations(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 502-523
Documentation:
/**
     * Do normalizations that introduce new siblings or parents.
     */
Method code:
502 |     private void doStatementNormalizations(
503 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
504 |       if (n.isLabel()) {
505 |         normalizeLabels(n);
506 |       }
507 | 
508 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs and LABELs, as all these
509 |       // are the only legal place for VARs and FOR statements.
510 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n) || n.isLabel()) {
511 |         extractForInitializer(n, null, null);
512 |       }
513 | 
514 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs, as all these
515 |       // are the only legal place for VARs.
516 |       if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
517 |         splitVarDeclarations(n);
518 |       }
519 | 
520 |       if (n.isFunction()) {
521 |         moveNamedFunctions(n.getLastChild());
522 |       }
523 |     }

===== M0535 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 463-483
Documentation:

Method code:
463 |     @Override
464 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
465 |       if (lineBreak) {
466 |         if (sawFunction) {
467 |           startNewLine();
468 |           sawFunction = false;
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
473 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
474 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
475 |       int len = code.length();
476 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
477 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
478 |         if (ch == ';') {
479 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
480 |         }
481 |       }
482 |       maybeCutLine();
483 |     }

===== M0553 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseAtScope(Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 351-372
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a parse tree recursively with a scope, starting at that scope's
   * root.
   */
Method code:
351 |   void traverseAtScope(Scope s) {
352 |     Node n = s.getRootNode();
353 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
354 |       // We need to do some extra magic to make sure that the scope doesn't
355 |       // get re-created when we dive into the function.
356 |       if (inputId == null) {
357 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
358 |       }
359 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
360 |       curNode = n;
361 |       pushScope(s);
362 | 
363 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
364 |       Node body = args.getNext();
365 |       traverseBranch(args, n);
366 |       traverseBranch(body, n);
367 | 
368 |       popScope();
369 |     } else {
370 |       traverseWithScope(n, s);
371 |     }
372 |   }
