/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
692 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
693 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
694 |     /*
695 |      * This is the case where:
696 |      *
697 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
698 |      * the script.
699 |      *
700 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
701 |      * the caller of the function.
702 |      *
703 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
704 |      * back to the caller of the function.
705 |      *
706 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
707 |      * asked to traverse.
708 |      *
709 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
710 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
711 |      */
712 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
713 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
714 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
715 |       return null;
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
719 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
720 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
721 |       case Token.IF:
722 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
723 |       case Token.CASE:
724 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
725 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
726 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
727 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
728 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
729 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
730 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
731 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
732 |           } else {
733 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
734 |           }
735 |         } else {
736 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
737 |         }
738 |         break;
739 |       case Token.FOR:
740 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
741 |           return parent;
742 |         } else {
743 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
744 |         }
745 |       case Token.WHILE:
746 |       case Token.DO:
747 |         return parent;
748 |       case Token.TRY:
749 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
750 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
751 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
752 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
753 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
754 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
755 |           }
756 |         // CATCH block.
757 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
758 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
759 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
760 |           } else {
761 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
762 |           }
763 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
764 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
765 |           if (cfa != null) {
766 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
767 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.ON_EX, finallyNode);
768 |             }
769 |           }
770 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
771 |         }
772 |     }
773 | 
774 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
775 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
776 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
777 | 
778 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
779 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
780 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
784 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
785 |     } else {
786 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transferred up the AST.
787 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
788 |     }
789 |   }

===== M0582 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 494-518
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
494 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
495 |     switch (n.getType()) {
496 |       case Token.STRING:
497 |       case Token.NUMBER:
498 |       case Token.NULL:
499 |       case Token.TRUE:
500 |       case Token.FALSE:
501 |         return true;
502 |       case Token.CAST:
503 |       case Token.NOT:
504 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
505 |       case Token.VOID:
506 |       case Token.NEG:
507 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
508 |       case Token.NAME:
509 |         String name = n.getString();
510 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
511 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
512 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
513 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
514 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
515 |     }
516 | 
517 |     return false;
518 |   }

===== M0587 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MaybeReachingVariableUse.computeMayUse(Node,Node,ReachingUses,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MaybeReachingVariableUse.java
Original range: 169-254
Documentation:

Method code:
169 |   private void computeMayUse(
170 |       Node n, Node cfgNode, ReachingUses output, boolean conditional) {
171 |     switch (n.getType()) {
172 | 
173 |       case Token.BLOCK:
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         return;
176 | 
177 |       case Token.NAME:
178 |         addToUseIfLocal(n.getString(), cfgNode, output);
179 |         return;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.WHILE:
182 |       case Token.DO:
183 |       case Token.IF:
184 |         computeMayUse(
185 |             NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
186 |         return;
187 | 
188 |       case Token.FOR:
189 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
190 |           computeMayUse(
191 |               NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), cfgNode, output, conditional);
192 |         } else {
193 |           // for(x in y) {...}
194 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
195 |           Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
196 |           if (lhs.isVar()) {
197 |             lhs = lhs.getLastChild(); // for(var x in y) {...}
198 |           }
199 |           if (lhs.isName() && !conditional) {
200 |             removeFromUseIfLocal(lhs.getString(), output);
201 |           }
202 |           computeMayUse(rhs, cfgNode, output, conditional);
203 |         }
204 |         return;
205 | 
206 |       case Token.AND:
207 |       case Token.OR:
208 |         computeMayUse(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
209 |         computeMayUse(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
210 |         return;
211 | 
212 |       case Token.HOOK:
213 |         computeMayUse(n.getLastChild(), cfgNode, output, true);
214 |         computeMayUse(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), cfgNode, output, true);
215 |         computeMayUse(n.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
216 |         return;
217 | 
218 |       case Token.VAR:
219 |         Node varName = n.getFirstChild();
220 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.hasChildren(), "AST should be normalized");
221 | 
222 |         if (varName.hasChildren()) {
223 |           computeMayUse(varName.getFirstChild(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
224 |           if (!conditional) {
225 |             removeFromUseIfLocal(varName.getString(), output);
226 |           }
227 |         }
228 |         return;
229 | 
230 |       default:
231 |         if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && n.getFirstChild().isName()) {
232 |           Node name = n.getFirstChild();
233 |           if (!conditional) {
234 |             removeFromUseIfLocal(name.getString(), output);
235 |           }
236 | 
237 |           // In case of a += "Hello". There is a read of a.
238 |           if (!n.isAssign()) {
239 |             addToUseIfLocal(name.getString(), cfgNode, output);
240 |           }
241 | 
242 |           computeMayUse(name.getNext(), cfgNode, output, conditional);
243 |         } else {
244 |           /*
245 |            * We want to traverse in reverse order because we want the LAST
246 |            * definition in the sub-tree....
247 |            * But we have no better way to traverse in reverse other :'(
248 |            */
249 |           for (Node c = n.getLastChild(); c != null; c = n.getChildBefore(c)) {
250 |             computeMayUse(c, cfgNode, output, conditional);
251 |           }
252 |         }
253 |     }
254 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 929-988
Documentation:

Method code:
929 |   private void toString(
930 |       StringBuilder sb,
931 |       boolean printSource,
932 |       boolean printAnnotations,
933 |       boolean printType) {
934 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
935 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
936 |       sb.append(' ');
937 |       sb.append(getString());
938 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
939 |       sb.append(' ');
940 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
941 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
942 |       // toStringTree.
943 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
944 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
945 |       } else {
946 |         sb.append(first.getString());
947 |       }
948 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
949 |       sb.append(' ');
950 |       sb.append(getDouble());
951 |     }
952 |     if (printSource) {
953 |       int lineno = getLineno();
954 |       if (lineno != -1) {
955 |         sb.append(' ');
956 |         sb.append(lineno);
957 |       }
958 |     }
959 | 
960 |     if (printAnnotations) {
961 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
962 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
963 |         int type = keys[i];
964 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
965 |         sb.append(" [");
966 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
967 |         sb.append(": ");
968 |         String value;
969 |         switch (type) {
970 |           default:
971 |             value = x.toString();
972 |             break;
973 |         }
974 |         sb.append(value);
975 |         sb.append(']');
976 |       }
977 |     }
978 | 
979 |     if (printType) {
980 |       if (jsType != null) {
981 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
982 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
983 |           sb.append(" : ");
984 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
985 |         }
986 |       }
987 |     }
988 |   }

===== M0601 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the Javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

===== M0603 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MarkNoSideEffectCalls.SetNoSideEffectCallProperty.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MarkNoSideEffectCalls.java
Original range: 175-197
Documentation:

Method code:
175 |     @Override
176 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
177 |       if (!node.isCall() && !node.isNew()) {
178 |         return;
179 |       }
180 | 
181 |       Collection<Definition> definitions =
182 |           defFinder.getDefinitionsReferencedAt(node.getFirstChild());
183 |       if (definitions == null) {
184 |         return;
185 |       }
186 | 
187 |       for (Definition def : definitions) {

===== M0632 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 830-857
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
831 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
832 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
833 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
834 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
835 |           return;
836 |         }
837 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
838 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
839 | 
840 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
841 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
842 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           } else {
844 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
845 |           }
846 |         } else { // Has a catch.
847 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
848 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
849 |             return;
850 |           } else {
851 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         lastJump = handler;
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |   }

===== M0635 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3035-3063
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3035 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3036 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3037 |     if (info == null) {
3038 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3039 |       if (parent == null) {
3040 |         return null;
3041 |       }
3042 | 
3043 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3044 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3045 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3046 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3047 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3048 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3049 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3050 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3051 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3052 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3053 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3054 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3055 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3056 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3057 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3058 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3059 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3060 |       }
3061 |     }
3062 |     return info;
3063 |   }

===== M0646 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1489-1538
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1489 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1490 |     if (type != node.getType()
1491 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1492 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1493 |       return false;
1494 |     }
1495 | 
1496 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1497 |       return false;
1498 |     }
1499 | 
1500 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1501 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1502 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1503 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1504 |         return false;
1505 |       }
1506 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1507 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1508 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1509 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1510 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1511 |           return false;
1512 |         }
1513 |       }
1514 | 
1515 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1516 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1517 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1518 |         return false;
1519 |       }
1520 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1521 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1522 |         return false;
1523 |       }
1524 |     }
1525 | 
1526 |     if (recurse) {
1527 |       Node n, n2;
1528 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1529 |            n != null;
1530 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1531 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1532 |           return false;
1533 |         }
1534 |       }
1535 |     }
1536 | 
1537 |     return true;
1538 |   }

===== M0647 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processTryStatement(TryStatement)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 1069-1102
Documentation:

Method code:
1069 |     @Override
1070 |     Node processTryStatement(TryStatement statementNode) {
1071 |       Node node = newNode(Token.TRY,
1072 |           transformBlock(statementNode.getTryBlock()));
1073 |       Node block = newNode(Token.BLOCK);
1074 |       boolean lineSet = false;
1075 | 
1076 |       for (CatchClause cc : statementNode.getCatchClauses()) {
1077 |         // Mark the enclosing block at the same line as the first catch
1078 |         // clause.
1079 |         if (lineSet == false) {
1080 |           block.setLineno(cc.getLineno());
1081 |           maybeSetLengthFrom(block, cc);
1082 |           lineSet = true;
1083 |         }
1084 |         block.addChildToBack(transform(cc));
1085 |       }
1086 |       node.addChildToBack(block);
1087 | 
1088 |       AstNode finallyBlock = statementNode.getFinallyBlock();
1089 |       if (finallyBlock != null) {
1090 |         node.addChildToBack(transformBlock(finallyBlock));
1091 |       }
1092 | 
1093 |       // If we didn't set the line on the catch clause, then
1094 |       // we've got an empty catch clause.  Set its line to be the same
1095 |       // as the finally block (to match Old Rhino's behavior.)
1096 |       if ((lineSet == false) && (finallyBlock != null)) {
1097 |         block.setLineno(finallyBlock.getLineno());
1098 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(block, finallyBlock);
1099 |       }
1100 | 
1101 |       return node;
1102 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()

Original range: 1548-1563
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1548 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1549 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1550 |       String name = getString();
1551 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1552 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1553 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1554 |       if (left == null) {
1555 |         return null;
1556 |       }
1557 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1558 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1559 |       return "this";
1560 |     } else {
1561 |       return null;
1562 |     }
1563 |   }

600 |       if (clauseNode.getCatchCondition() != null) {
601 |         errorReporter.error(
602 |             "Catch clauses are not supported",
603 |             sourceName,
604 |             clauseNode.getCatchCondition().getLineno(), "", 0);
605 |       }
606 |       node.addChildToBack(transformBlock(clauseNode.getBody()));
607 |       return node;
608 |     }

===== M0671 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1937-1939
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1937 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1938 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1939 |   }

/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.

/**
   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments and for handling
   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.
   * @param irNode
   * @return A JsDocInfoParser. Will contain either fileoverview JsDoc, or
   *     normal JsDoc, or no JsDoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).

===== M0709 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 654-742
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
654 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
655 |     if (this == that) {
656 |       return true;
657 |     }
658 | 
659 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
660 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
661 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
662 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
663 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
664 |         // with everyone.
665 |         return true;
666 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
667 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
668 |         // both unknown.
669 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
670 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
671 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
672 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
673 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
674 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
675 |         return false;
676 |       }
677 |     }
678 | 
679 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
680 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
681 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
682 |     }
683 | 
684 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
685 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
686 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
687 |     }
688 | 
689 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
690 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
691 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
692 |     }
693 | 
694 |     ParameterizedType thisParamType = toMaybeParameterizedType();
695 |     ParameterizedType thatParamType = that.toMaybeParameterizedType();
696 |     if (thisParamType != null || thatParamType != null) {
697 |       // Check if one type is parameterized, but the other is not.
698 |       boolean paramsMatch = false;
699 |       if (thisParamType != null && thatParamType != null) {
700 |         paramsMatch = thisParamType.getParameterType().checkEquivalenceHelper(
701 |             thatParamType.getParameterType(), eqMethod);
702 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY) {
703 |         paramsMatch = false;
704 |       } else {
705 |         // If one of the type parameters is unknown, but the other is not,
706 |         // then we consider these the same for the purposes of data flow
707 |         // and invariance.
708 |         paramsMatch = true;
709 |       }
710 | 
711 |       JSType thisRootType = thisParamType == null ?
712 |           this : thisParamType.getReferencedTypeInternal();
713 |       JSType thatRootType = thatParamType == null ?
714 |           that : thatParamType.getReferencedTypeInternal();
715 |       return paramsMatch &&
716 |           thisRootType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatRootType, eqMethod);
717 |     }
718 | 
719 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
720 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
721 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
722 |     }
723 | 
724 |     // Unbox other proxies.
725 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
726 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
727 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
728 |               that, eqMethod);
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
732 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
733 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
734 |           eqMethod);
735 |     }
736 | 
737 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
738 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
739 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
740 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
741 |     return this == that;
742 |   }

===== M0714 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processStringLiteral(StringLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 1001-1028
Documentation:

Method code:
1001 |     @Override
1002 |     Node processStringLiteral(StringLiteral literalNode) {
1003 |       String value = literalNode.getValue();
1004 |       Node n = newStringNode(value);
1005 |       if (value.indexOf('\u000B') != -1) {
1006 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): In JavaScript, there are 3 ways to
1007 |         // represent a vertical tab: \v, \x0B, \u000B.
1008 |         // The \v notation was added later, and is not understood
1009 |         // on IE. So we need to preserve it as-is. This is really
1010 |         // obnoxious, because we do not have a good way to represent
1011 |         // how the original string was encoded without making the
1012 |         // representation of strings much more complicated.
1013 |         //
1014 |         // To handle this, we look at the original source test, and
1015 |         // mark the string as \v-encoded or not. If a string is
1016 |         // \v encoded, then all the vertical tabs in that string
1017 |         // will be encoded with a \v.
1018 |         int start = literalNode.getAbsolutePosition();
1019 |         int end = start + literalNode.getLength();
1020 |         if (start < sourceString.length() &&
1021 |             (sourceString.substring(
1022 |                  start, Math.min(sourceString.length(), end))
1023 |              .indexOf("\\v") != -1)) {
1024 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.SLASH_V, true);
1025 |         }
1026 |       }
1027 |       return n;
1028 |     }

/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1425 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1426 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1427 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1428 |     }
1429 | 
1430 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1431 |     Node n, n2;
1432 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1433 |          res == null && n != null;
1434 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1435 |       if (node2 == null) {
1436 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1437 |       }
1438 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1439 |       if (res != null) {
1440 |         return res;
1441 |       }
1442 |     }
1443 |     return res;
1444 |   }

Original range: 418-448
Documentation:
/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
418 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
419 |       Preconditions.checkState(
420 |           n.isName()
421 |           || n.isString()
422 |           || n.isStringKey()
423 |           || n.isGetterDef()
424 |           || n.isSetterDef());
425 | 
426 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
427 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
428 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
429 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent);
430 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
431 |           (parent.isGetProp() &&
432 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
433 |       if (n.isName() || isProperty) {
434 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
435 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
436 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
437 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
438 |           if (assertOnChange) {
439 |             String name = n.getString();
440 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
441 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
442 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
443 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
444 |           }
445 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
446 |         }
447 |       }
448 |     }

===== M0736 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.has(Node,Predicate<Node>,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2677-2695
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the predicate is true for the node or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2677 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2678 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2679 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2680 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2681 |       return true;
2682 |     }
2683 | 
2684 |     if (!traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2685 |       return false;
2686 |     }
2687 | 
2688 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2689 |       if (has(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred)) {
2690 |         return true;
2691 |       }
2692 |     }
2693 | 
2694 |     return false;
2695 |   }

===== M0739 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.anonymous_L87.apply(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 88-117
Documentation:

Method code:
88 |       @Override
89 |       public boolean apply(Node n) {
90 |         // When the node is null it means, we reached the implicit return
91 |         // where the function returns (possibly without an return statement)
92 |         if (n == null) {
93 |           return false;
94 |         }
95 | 
96 |         // TODO(user): We only care about calls to functions that
97 |         // passes one of the dependent variable to a non-side-effect free
98 |         // function.
99 |         if (n.isCall() && NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
100 |           return true;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         if (n.isNew() && NodeUtil.constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
104 |           return true;
105 |         }
106 | 
107 |         if (n.isDelProp()) {
108 |           return true;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
112 |           if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && apply(c)) {
113 |             return true;
114 |           }
115 |         }
116 |         return false;
117 |       }

===== M0747 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 267-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Performs a single flow through a node.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if the flow state differs from the previous state.
   */
Method code:
267 |   protected boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
268 |     FlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
269 |     if (isForward()) {
270 |       L outBefore = state.out;
271 |       state.out = flowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
272 |       return !outBefore.equals(state.out);
273 |     } else {
274 |       L inBefore = state.in;
275 |       state.in = flowThrough(node.getValue(), state.out);
276 |       return !inBefore.equals(state.in);
277 |     }
278 |   }

===== M0753 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1409-1445
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1409 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1410 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1411 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1412 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1413 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1414 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1415 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1416 |           continue;
1417 |         }
1418 |       }
1419 | 
1420 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1421 | 
1422 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1423 |       if (n == null) {
1424 |         continue;
1425 |       }
1426 | 
1427 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1428 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1429 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1430 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1431 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1432 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1433 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1434 | 
1435 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1436 | 
1437 |         externs.add(input);
1438 |         staleInputs = true;
1439 |       }
1440 |     }
1441 | 
1442 |     if (staleInputs) {
1443 |       repartitionInputs();
1444 |     }
1445 |   }

===== M0787 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

===== M0792 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

/**
     * Set the def node
     *
     * @param n A node that has a corresponding CFG node in the CFG.
     */
Method code:
444 |     private void getDefinition(Node n, Node parent) {
445 |       AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback gatherCb =
446 |         new AbstractCfgNodeTraversalCallback() {
447 | 
448 |         @Override
449 |         public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
450 |           switch (n.getType()) {
451 |             case Token.NAME:
452 |               if (n.getString().equals(varName) && n.hasChildren()) {

462 |               return;
463 |           }
464 |         }
465 |       };
466 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, n, gatherCb);
467 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)

Original range: 309-316
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
310 |     if (c <= 127) {
311 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
312 |     } else {
313 |       return c == 0xA0
314 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
315 |     }
316 |   }

/**
   * Gets a list of nodes that may be using the value assigned to {@code name}
   * in {@code defNode}. {@code defNode} must be one of the control flow graph
   * nodes.
   *
   * @param name name of the variable. It can only be names of local variable
   *     that are not function parameters, escaped variables or variables
   *     declared in catch.
   * @param defNode The list of upward exposed use for the variable.
   */

296 |   Collection<Node> getUses(String name, Node defNode) {
297 |     GraphNode<Node, Branch> n = getCfg().getNode(defNode);
298 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(n);
299 |     FlowState<ReachingUses> state = n.getAnnotation();
300 |     return state.getOut().mayUseMap.get(jsScope.getVar(name));
301 |   }

===== M0820 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefinitionsRemover.java
Original range: 37-65
Documentation:
/**
   * @return an {@link Definition} object if the node contains a definition or
   *     {@code null} otherwise.
   */
Method code:
37 |   static Definition getDefinition(Node n, boolean isExtern) {
38 |     // TODO(user): Since we have parent pointers handy. A lot of constructors
39 |     // can be simplified.
40 | 
41 |     // This logic must match #isDefinitionNode
42 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
43 |     if (parent == null) {
44 |       return null;
45 |     }
46 | 
47 |     if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n) && n.hasChildren()) {
48 |       return new VarDefinition(n, isExtern);
49 |     } else if (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
50 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
51 |         return new NamedFunctionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
52 |       } else if (!n.getString().equals("")) {
53 |         return new FunctionExpressionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
54 |       }
55 |     } else if (parent.isAssign() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
56 |       return new AssignmentDefinition(parent, isExtern);
57 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
58 |       return new ObjectLiteralPropertyDefinition(parent, n, n.getFirstChild(),
59 |           isExtern);
60 |     } else if (parent.isParamList()) {
61 |       Node function = parent.getParent();
62 |       return new FunctionArgumentDefinition(function, n, isExtern);
63 |     }
64 |     return null;
65 |   }

===== M0827 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1040-1163
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape JavaScript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1040 |   private String strEscape(
1041 |       String s,
1042 |       char quote,
1043 |       String doublequoteEscape,
1044 |       String singlequoteEscape,
1045 |       String backslashEscape,
1046 |       CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder,
1047 |       boolean useSlashV,
1048 |       boolean isRegexp) {
1049 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1050 |     sb.append(quote);
1051 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1052 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1053 |       switch (c) {
1054 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
1055 |         case '\u000B':
1056 |           if (useSlashV) {
1057 |             sb.append("\\v");
1058 |           } else {
1059 |             sb.append("\\x0B");
1060 |           }
1061 |           break;
1062 |         // From the SingleEscapeCharacter grammar production.
1063 |         case '\b': sb.append("\\b"); break;
1064 |         case '\f': sb.append("\\f"); break;
1065 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1066 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1067 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1068 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1069 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1070 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1071 | 
1072 |         // From LineTerminators (ES5 Section 7.3, Table 3)
1073 |         case '\u2028': sb.append("\\u2028"); break;
1074 |         case '\u2029': sb.append("\\u2029"); break;
1075 | 
1076 |         case '=':
1077 |           // '=' is a syntactically signficant regexp character.
1078 |           if (trustedStrings || isRegexp) {
1079 |             sb.append(c);
1080 |           } else {
1081 |             sb.append("\\x3d");
1082 |           }
1083 |           break;
1084 | 
1085 |         case '&':
1086 |           if (trustedStrings || isRegexp) {
1087 |             sb.append(c);
1088 |           } else {
1089 |             sb.append("\\x26");
1090 |           }
1091 |           break;
1092 | 
1093 |         case '>':
1094 |           if (!trustedStrings && !isRegexp) {
1095 |             sb.append(GT_ESCAPED);
1096 |             break;
1097 |           }
1098 | 
1099 |           // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1100 |           //
1101 |           // This is just to prevent developers from shooting themselves in the
1102 |           // foot, and does not provide the level of security that you get
1103 |           // with trustedString == false.
1104 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1105 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1106 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1107 |             sb.append(GT_ESCAPED);
1108 |           } else {
1109 |             sb.append(c);
1110 |           }
1111 |           break;
1112 |         case '<':
1113 |           if (!trustedStrings && !isRegexp) {
1114 |             sb.append(LT_ESCAPED);
1115 |             break;
1116 |           }
1117 | 
1118 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1119 |           // As above, this is just to prevent developers from doing this
1120 |           // accidentally.
1121 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1122 | 
1123 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1124 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1125 | 
1126 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1127 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1128 |             sb.append(LT_ESCAPED);
1129 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1130 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1131 |             sb.append(LT_ESCAPED);
1132 |           } else {
1133 |             sb.append(c);
1134 |           }
1135 |           break;
1136 |         default:
1137 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1138 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1139 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1140 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1141 |               sb.append(c);
1142 |             } else {
1143 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1144 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1145 |             }
1146 |           } else {
1147 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight Latin characters
1148 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1149 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1150 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1151 |               sb.append(c);
1152 |             } else {
1153 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some JS parsers,
1154 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1155 |               // so we play it safe and Unicode escape them.
1156 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1157 |             }
1158 |           }
1159 |       }
1160 |     }
1161 |     sb.append(quote);
1162 |     return sb.toString();
1163 |   }

Original range: 212-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0847 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.checkLeftOf(Node,Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 521-532
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an expression by its root and sub-expression n, return true if there
   * the predicate is true for some expression on the left of n.
   *
   * Example:
   *
   * Checked(), Checked(), n, NotChecked(), NotChecked();
   */
Method code:
521 |   private static boolean checkLeftOf(
522 |       Node n, Node expressionRoot, Predicate<Node> predicate) {
523 |     for (Node p = n.getParent(); p != expressionRoot; p = p.getParent()) {
524 |       for (Node cur = p.getParent().getFirstChild(); cur != p;
525 |           cur = cur.getNext()) {
526 |         if (predicate.apply(cur)) {
527 |           return true;
528 |         }
529 |       }
530 |     }
531 |     return false;
532 |   }

===== M0848 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo,AstNode,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 289-341
Documentation:

Method code:
289 |   private void validateTypeAnnotations(
290 |       JSDocInfo info, AstNode node, Node irNode) {
291 |     if (info.hasType()) {
292 |       boolean valid = false;
293 |       switch (node.getType()) {
294 |         // Casts are valid
295 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.LP:
296 |           valid = node instanceof ParenthesizedExpression;
297 |           break;
298 |         // Variable declarations are valid
299 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.VAR:
300 |           valid = true;
301 |           break;
302 |         // Function declarations are valid
303 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.FUNCTION:
304 |           FunctionNode fnNode = (FunctionNode)node;
305 |           valid = fnNode.getFunctionType() == FunctionNode.FUNCTION_STATEMENT;
306 |           break;
307 |         // Object literal properties and catch declarations are valid.
308 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NAME:
309 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty
310 |               || node.getParent() instanceof CatchClause
311 |               || node.getParent() instanceof FunctionNode;
312 |           break;
313 |         // Object literal properties are valid
314 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GET:
315 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.SET:
316 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NUMBER:
317 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.STRING:
318 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty;
319 |           break;
320 | 
321 |         // Property assignments are valid, if at the root of an expression.
322 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.ASSIGN:
323 |           if (node instanceof Assignment) {
324 |             valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent())
325 |                 && isPropAccess(((Assignment)node).getLeft());
326 |           }
327 |           break;
328 | 
329 |         // Property definitions are valid, if at the root of an expression.
330 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETPROP:
331 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETELEM:
332 |           valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent());
333 |           break;
334 |       }
335 |       if (!valid) {
336 |         errorReporter.warning(MISPLACED_TYPE_ANNOTATION,
337 |             sourceName,
338 |             node.getLineno(), "", 0);
339 |       }
340 |     }
341 |   }

===== M0854 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1165-1185
Documentation:

Method code:
1165 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1166 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1167 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1168 |       return s;
1169 |     }
1170 | 
1171 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1172 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1173 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1174 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1175 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1176 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1177 |       // crazy.
1178 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1179 |         sb.append(c);
1180 |       } else {
1181 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1182 |       }
1183 |     }
1184 |     return sb.toString();
1185 |   }

235 |     @Override
236 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
237 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
238 |       if (n.isName()) {
239 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
240 |           return;
241 |         }
242 | 
243 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
244 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top-level variable.
245 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
246 |         if (var != null) {

248 |         }
249 |

251 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
252 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
253 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
254 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
255 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {

264 |         }
265 |       }
266 |     }

===== M0873 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0875 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

===== M0885 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 709-791
Documentation:

Method code:
709 |     @Override
710 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
711 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
712 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
713 |       if (name == null) {
714 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
715 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
716 |           errorReporter.error(
717 |             "unnamed function statement",
718 |             sourceName,
719 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
720 | 
721 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
722 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
723 |         }
724 |         name = new Name();
725 |         name.setIdentifier("");
726 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
727 |       }
728 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
729 |       Node newName = transform(name);
730 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
731 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
732 |         // declaration.
733 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
734 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
735 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
736 |         // same line as the function keyword.
737 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
738 |             functionNode.getLp();
739 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
740 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
741 |       }
742 | 
743 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
744 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
745 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
746 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
747 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
748 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
749 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
750 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
751 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
752 |       if (fnName != null) {
753 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
754 |       } else {
755 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
756 |       }
757 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
758 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
759 | 
760 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
761 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
762 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
763 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
764 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
765 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
766 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
767 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
768 |         } else {
769 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
770 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
771 |           // (an error has already been reported).
772 |           Preconditions.checkState(
773 |               config.isIdeMode
774 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
775 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
776 |         }
777 |       }
778 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
779 | 
780 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
781 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
782 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
783 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
784 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
785 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
786 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
787 |       }
788 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
789 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
790 |      return node;
791 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // parameterized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isParameterizedType() || current.isParameterizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isParameterizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isParameterizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isParameterizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isParameterizedType());
224 |                 ParameterizedType parameterizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeParameterizedType();
225 |                 ParameterizedType parameterizedCurrent = current.toMaybeParameterizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (parameterizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(parameterizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   JSType alternateTypeParameter = parameterizedAlternate.getParameterType();
229 |                   JSType currentTypeParameter = parameterizedCurrent.getParameterType();
230 |                   if (currentTypeParameter.isEquivalentTo(parameterizedCurrent)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the parameterized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = parameterizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both parameterized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,

270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0927 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FlowSensitiveInlineVariables.java
Original range: 124-170
Documentation:

Method code:
124 |   @Override
125 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
126 | 
127 |     if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
128 |       return; // Don't even brother. All global variables are likely escaped.
129 |     }
130 | 
131 |     if (LiveVariablesAnalysis.MAX_VARIABLES_TO_ANALYZE <
132 |         t.getScope().getVarCount()) {
133 |       return;
134 |     }
135 | 
136 |     // Compute the forward reaching definition.
137 |     ControlFlowAnalysis cfa = new ControlFlowAnalysis(compiler, false, true);
138 |     // Process the body of the function.
139 |     Preconditions.checkState(t.getScopeRoot().isFunction());
140 |     cfa.process(null, t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild());
141 |     cfg = cfa.getCfg();
142 |     reachingDef = new MustBeReachingVariableDef(cfg, t.getScope(), compiler);
143 |     reachingDef.analyze();
144 |     candidates = Lists.newLinkedList();
145 | 
146 |     // Using the forward reaching definition search to find all the inline
147 |     // candidates
148 |     new NodeTraversal(compiler, new GatherCandiates()).traverse(
149 |         t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild());
150 | 
151 |     // Compute the backward reaching use. The CFG can be reused.
152 |     reachingUses = new MaybeReachingVariableUse(cfg, t.getScope(), compiler);
153 |     reachingUses.analyze();
154 |     for (Candidate c : candidates) {
155 |       if (c.canInline()) {
156 |         c.inlineVariable();
157 | 
158 |         // If definition c has dependencies, then inlining it may have
159 |         // introduced new dependencies for our other inlining candidates.
160 |         //
161 |         // MustBeReachingVariableDef uses this dependency graph in its
162 |         // analysis, so some of these candidates may no longer be valid.
163 |         // We keep track of when the variable dependency graph changed
164 |         // so that we can back off appropriately.
165 |         if (!c.defMetadata.depends.isEmpty()) {
166 |           inlinedNewDependencies.add(t.getScope().getVar(c.varName));
167 |         }
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

===== M0931 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 511-546
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
514 | 
515 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
516 | 
517 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
518 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
519 | 
520 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
521 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
522 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     curNode = n;
526 |     pushScope(n);
527 | 
528 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
529 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
530 |       // scope.
531 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
535 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
536 | 
537 |     // Args
538 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
539 | 
540 |     // Body
541 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
542 |             body.isBlock(), body);
543 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
544 | 
545 |     popScope();
546 |   }

/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
